COMPLIANCE WITH THE CORPORATE                                           遵守企業管治守則

governance practices to protect Shareholders’ rights and enhance        障股東權益以及提升企業價值及責任制度。
corporate value and accountability.

The Company maintains a high standard of corporate governance           本公司維持高標準的企業管治常規。董事會
practices. The Board does not take risks to make short-term gains       不會冒險為獲取短期收益而犧牲長遠目標。
at the expense of the long-term objectives. The Company has             本公司自上市日期起已採納聯交所上市規則
adopted the CG Code contained in Appendix C1 (formerly known            附錄C1（前稱附錄十四）所載企業管治守則作
as Appendix 14) to the Listing Rules on Stock Exchange as its own       為其自身的企業管治守則。自上市日期起至
code of corporate governance since the Listing Date. Save and           2023年12月31日止期間，除下文所披露偏
except for the deviation from code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code       離企業管治守則的守則條文第C.2.1條外，本
as disclosed below, the Company has complied with all the code          公司已遵守企業管治守則所載的所有守則條
provisions in the CG Code for the period from the Listing Date to       文。
December 31, 2023.

The following is a summary of the principal terms of the Pre-          以下為經2021年6月1日（「採納日期」）董事

Board dated June 1, 2021 (the “Adoption Date”) and amended on          訂的首次公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃的
February 20, 2023. The Pre-IPO RSU Scheme is not subject to the        主要條款概要。首次公開發售前受限制股份
provisions of Chapter 17 of the Listing Rules as it does not involve   單位計劃毋須遵守上市規則第十七章條文的
any grant of share options or awards by our Company after the          規定，原因是其並不涉及本公司於上市後任
Listing.                                                               何授出購股權或獎勵的行為。

a.   Purpose                                                           a.   目的
     The purpose of the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme is to reward the                 首次公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃的
     grantees for their services and contribution to the success            目的乃為嘉許承授人對本集團的成功作
     of the Group, and to provide incentives to them to further             出服務及貢獻，並為彼等對本集團的進
     contribute to the Group.                                               一步貢獻提供獎勵。

b.   RSU Awards                                                        b.   受限制股份單位獎勵
     An award of RSUs under the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme                          首次公開發售前受限制股份單位計劃項

     contingent right upon vesting of the Award to obtain either            予參與者（「受限制股份單位參與者」）
     Shares or an equivalent value in cash with reference to the            一項或有權利，在獎勵歸屬時獲取經董
     market value of the Shares underlying such Award on or                 事會參考歸屬日期或前後有關獎勵涉及
     about the date of vesting, as determined by the Board in its           的股份的市值而全權酌情釐定的股份或
     sole discretion.                                                       等值現金。

c.   Participants                                                      c.   參與者

     the Group who the Board considers, in its sole discretion, has         董事會全權酌情釐定認為已經或將會對

     to receive Awards under the Pre-IPO RSU Scheme at its                  格人士」）。董事會酌情選定獲取首次公
     discretion.                                                            開發售前受限制股份單位計劃項下獎勵
                                                                            的受限制股份單位合資格人士。

Duties, Responsibilities and Contributions of the Board                董事會及高級管理層的職責、責任及貢
and Senior Management                                                  獻
The Board is the core function of the Company’s corporate              董事會乃本公司企業管治架構的核心部分。
governance structure. The principal responsibilities of the Board      董事會的主要責任為設立企業管治之整體框
are to set an overall framework of corporate governance within         架及監察本公司之運營，高級管理層在企業
which senior management conducts business and to monitor               管治之整體框架內開展業務。本公司已經制
the Company’s operations. The Company has formulated overall           定企業管治的整體框架，包含內部指引、內
framework of corporate governance, containing internal guidelines,     部控制政策及程序。
internal control policies and procedures.

The Board has formulated a clear policy that stipulates the            董事會已制定清晰政策，規定高級管理層應
circumstances under which senior management should report              向董事會匯報的情形，以及在代表本公司
to the Board and obtain approval from the Board before making          作出任何決定或任何承諾前應獲得董事會批
any decisions or entering into any commitments on behalf of the        准。董事會定期檢討企業管治常規，並於適
Company. The Board regularly reviews corporate governance              當時更新。
practices and updates them as and when appropriate.

The Board, through its committees, directly and indirectly leads and   董事會透過其委員會直接及間接領導及指導
guides the management, formulates strategies and oversees their        管理層，制定策略並監督其實施，監督本集
implementation, monitors the Group’s operational and financial         團的運營及財務表現，並確保建立健全的內
performance, and ensures that sound internal control and risk          部控制及風險管理系統。
management systems are in place.

All Directors, including non-executive Directors and independent       所有董事，包括非執行董事及獨立非執行董
non-executive Directors, have brought a wide spectrum of valuable      事，均為董事會帶來廣泛的寶貴商業經驗、
business experience, knowledge and professionalism to the              知識及專業精神，使其能夠高效及有效地運
Board for its efficient and effective operations. The independent      作。獨立非執行董事負責確保本公司有高標
non-executive Directors are responsible for ensuring a high            準的監管報告，並在董事會對公司舉措及運
standard of regulatory reporting of the Company and playing a          營作出有效的獨立判斷時發揮平衡作用。
balanced role for the Board in exercising effective independent
judgment on the Company’s initiatives and operations.

All Directors have full and timely access to all information of the    所有董事均充分及時獲得本公司的所有資
Company and may, upon request, seek independent professional           訊，並在適當的情況下應要求尋求獨立的專
advice in appropriate circumstances at the Company’s expenses          業意見，費用由本公司承擔，以履行其對本
for discharging their duties to the Company. Upon reviewing            公司的職責。經檢討上述措施的實施及成效
the implementation and effectiveness of the above, the Board           後，董事會信納該機制可確保向董事會提供
is satisfied that such mechanism can ensure the availability of        獨立觀點及意見。
independent views and input to the Board.

experience, talents, skills, knowledge, cultural and educational          種族及服務年期。
background, gender, age, ethnicity and length of service.

development and finance experiences.

CONTROL
The Board recognizes its responsibility for risk management             董事會確認其對風險管理及內部控制制度及
and internal control systems and reviews their effectiveness.           審查其有效性的責任。該等制度旨在管理而
These systems are designed to manage, rather than eliminate,            不是消除無法實現業務目標的風險，並只能
the risk of failure to achieve business objectives and can only         提供合理而非絕對的保證，以防止重大誤報
provide reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against material        或損失。
misstatement or loss.

The Board is fully responsible for assessing and determining            董事會全面負責評估及確定其在實現本公司
the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take in achieving   策略目標過程中願意承擔的風險的性質及程
the Company’s strategic objectives and for establishing and             度，並建立及維護適當有效的風險管理及內
maintaining appropriate and effective risk management and internal      部控制機制。
control mechanisms.

and overseeing their design, implementation and monitoring of risk      等對風險管理及內部控制系統的設計、實施

and risk management systems. The Company has established an             獨立的內部審核部門直接向審核委員會匯報。

ESG MISSION AND VISION                                                  ESG使命及願景
As people’s awareness of climate change, human rights, and social       隨著人們對氣候變化、人權及社會公益等話
welfare continues to rise, sustainable development has become           題的關注度持續上升，可持續發展具象化已
an inevitable global trend. Therefore, the Group responds to the        成為全球必然趨勢。因此，集團順應可持續
trend of sustainable development by placing high importance on          發展趨勢，高度重視並堅定履行作為企業公
and steadfastly fulfilling its ESG responsibilities as a corporate      民的ESG責任，致力於實現ESG使命。我們
citizen, committed to achieving its ESG mission. We always adhere       始終堅守初心，將業務發展與ESG相結合，
to our original intentions, integrating business development with       力求在二者之間取得完美的平衡。我們將持
ESG, and strive to achieve a perfect balance between the two. We        續以實際行動為全球可持續性發展貢獻力
will continue to contribute to global suitabilities through practical   量，為社會帶來正面影響。
actions, bringing positive impacts to society.

RISK MANAGEMENT                                                          風險管理
The Group has already invested and will continue to invest               本集團已投入並將於未來持續投入大量資
substantial resources in establishing and continuously improving the     源，用於設立並持續完善集團風險管理體
Group’s risk management system to ensure the stability and safety        系，以確保業務運作的穩健與安全。我們致
of its operations. We are committed to building and maintaining a        力建立與維護由適合集團業務運營的政策和
risk management and internal control system composed of policies         程序所組成的風險管理及內部控制系統，其
and procedures suitable for the Group’s business operations,             中應用人工智能監督管理、財務報告風險管
incorporating the supervision and management of artificial               理和內部控制風險管理，細分為關聯方交易
intelligence, financial reporting risk management, and internal          風險管理、數據及內部控制風險管理、數據
control risk management. This includes subcategories such as             及信息安全管理以及僱員資質及行為合規風
related party transaction risk management, data and internal control     險管理等重要方面，確保每一環節都得到有
risk management, data and information security management,               效監控。
as well as employee qualification and behavioral compliance risk
management, among other important aspects, to ensure effective

principle of continuous improvement, we constantly optimize              斷優化風險管理及內部控制系統，確保其在

As a leading online investment decision-making solution provider           作為一家中國領先的在線投資決策解決方案
in China, we focus on the online investor content service market           提供商，我們專注於中國在線投資者內容服
in China. The nature of the Group’s business and its operating             務市場。我們業務性質及營運方式並無對環
methods do not have a significant negative impact on the                   境造成重大負面影響。基於環境狀況、社會
environment. Based on environmental conditions, social concerns,           關注及本集團責任意識，本集團積極履行社

in its daily operations, and contributes to social development.

RESOURCES MANAGEMENT                                                   資源管理
The Group attaches great importance to the impact of resource          本集團高度重視資源消耗對環境的影響，持
consumption on the environment and is continuously committed           續致力於資源保護。本集團主要營運地點位
to resource protection. The Group’s principal place of business is     於中國上海市青浦區，暫無取水問題及地域
located in Qingpu District, Shanghai, the PRC, where there is no       水源壓力。本年度水資源消耗密度為24.79
issue on sourcing water or geographical water stress for the time      噸╱人，較去年有小幅下降，主要原因為本
being. The water resources consumption density was 24.79 tons/         年度集團業務增長，辦公室營運、設備運行
person during the year, a slight decrease as compared to last year,    及維護時間有所增長，但集團節水措施、宣
mainly due to the expansion of the Group’s business during the         傳落實到位，集團員工也有意識的珍惜水資
year, with an increase in office operation, equipment operation        源，盡量回收利用水資源。
and maintenance time. However, the Group’s water conservation
measures and promotion were well implemented, and the Group’s
employees were also conscious of valuing water resources and
recycling them as much as possible.

higher occupational hazard risks. In order to ensure the safety of      工作環境安全，有效防範潛在的職業危害，

      employees brought by such situations in advance.                      給僱員帶來的安全威脅。

Acceptance of           The After-Sales Department receives customer complaints initiated through various
Complaint               channels such as on-site visits, complaint hotlines, email, mail, or referrals from other
                        departments. Complaints are categorized based on their severity, and customer complaint
                        files are created. Upon receiving a complaint, the After-Sales Department aims to conduct
                        a customer follow-up within one business day to understand the relevant details.
投訴受理                    售後部受理客戶通過上門、投訴專線、電子郵箱、信件、其他部門接轉等渠道發起的投訴，
                        並根據投訴情況劃分投訴級別、建立客戶投訴檔案。售後部在接到投訴後，將於1個工作日內
                        進行客戶回訪，瞭解相關情況。
Complaint Verification The After-Sales Department promptly verifies the complaint details and provides feedback
and Investigation       to the Compliance Department for investigation. Relevant departments are expected to
                        actively cooperate with the After-Sales Department in verification and the Compliance
                        Department in reviewing the complaints. The Compliance Department is responsible for
                        issuing compliance reports regarding customer complaints and notifying the After-Sales
                        Department and other relevant departments.
投訴調查核實                  售後部及時核實投訴情況，並反饋至合規部調查；相關部門應積極配合售後部核實及合規部
                        審查；針對客戶投訴，合規部應出具合規報告，通報售後部及其他相關部門。
Handling Complaint      The After-Sales Department promptly follows up on the status of handling and maintains
                        relevant records. Based on the verification results, compliance investigations, and relevant
                        Group policies, the department actively engages in communication and negotiation with
                        customers. The After-Sales Department addresses customer complaints through methods
                        such as appeasing the customers and providing explanations, re-assigning service
                        personnel, modifying services, issuing product refunds, offering product extensions,
                        product replacements, or providing complimentary gifts to appease customer emotions.
                        Relevant departments are expected to actively and closely cooperate with the After-Sales
                        Department to ensure effective complaint handling and prevent the escalation of complaint
                        risks.
投訴處理                    售後部及時跟進處理情況並做相關記錄，並根據核實情況、合規調查情況及集團相關制度與
                        客戶積極溝通協商；售後部通過安撫解釋、調整服務人員、調整服務、產品退費、產品延
                        期、更換產品、贈送禮品等方式處理客戶投訴，安撫客戶情緒；相關部門需積極、密切配合
                        售後部做好客戶投訴處理工作，並防止投訴風險擴散。
Pursuing Accountability The Compliance Department, in accordance with relevant Group policies, makes
Upon Determination of determinations of non-compliance against relevant departments or individuals. Depending
Non-Compliance          on the severity of the non-compliance, corresponding accountabilities are pursued, and the
                        outcomes of the handling are communicated to the relevant departments.
違規判定追責                  合規部根據集團相關制度對相關部門或人員進行違規認定，視違規情況追究其相應責任，並
                        將處理結果通報至有關部門。
Handling situation      The After-Sales Department maintains timely communication with relevant service
feedback                departments, providing updates on customer status and the progress of complaint
                        handling. The relevant service departments engage in timely reflection on the identified
                        issues and adjust customer service approach and content accordingly.
處理情況反饋                  售後部與相關服務部門及時溝通、回饋客戶狀況與投訴處理情況；相關服務部門及時反思相
                        關問題，並調整客戶服務方式與內容。
File Management         The After-Sales Department creates and maintains customer complaint files, which are
                        properly safeguarded by dedicated personnel.
檔案管理                    售後部建立並保管客戶的投訴檔案，由專人妥善保管。

of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the consolidated      全面收益表，合併權益變動表及合併現金流

accounting policy information and other explanatory information.

information comprises all the information included in the Annual       於年度報告內的所有資料，但不包括合併財
Report, other than the consolidated financial statements and our       務報表及我們的核數師報告。
auditor’s report thereon.

Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements does not          我們對合併財務報表的意見並不涵蓋其他資
cover the other information and we do not express any form of          料，我們亦不對該等其他資料發表任何形式
assurance conclusion thereon.                                          的鑒證結論。


In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial             就我們對合併財務報表的審核而言，我們的
statements, our responsibility is to read the other information and,   責任是閱讀其他資料，在此過程中，考慮其
in doing so, consider whether the other information is materially      他資料是否與合併財務報表或我們在審核過
inconsistent with the consolidated financial statements or our         程中所了解的情況存在重大抵觸或者似乎存
knowledge obtained in the audit or otherwise appears to be             在重大錯誤陳述的情況。
materially misstated.

If, based on the work we have performed, we conclude that there is     基於我們已執行的工作，倘我們認為其他資
a material misstatement of this other information, we are required     料存在重大錯誤陳述，我們須報告該事實。
to report that fact. We have nothing to report in this regard.         在這方面，我們並無任何報告。

    (d)   Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                       (d)   附屬公司及非控股權益
          Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the Group. The                 附屬公司指本集團控制的實體。
          Group controls an entity when it is exposed to, or has                 倘本集團因參與該實體而承擔風
          rights to, variable returns from its involvement with the              險或有權享有可變回報，並能通
          entity and has the ability to affect those returns through             過對該實體行使權力影響該等回
          its power over the entity. The financial statements of                 報，則本集團控制該實體。附屬
          subsidiaries are included in the consolidated financial                公司的財務報表自控制權開始之
          statements from the date on which control commences                    日起至控制權終止之日均包含於
          until the date on which control ceases.                                合併財務報表中。


          Intra-Group balances and transactions, and any                         集團內部餘額及交易，以及集團
          unrealised income and expenses (except for foreign                     內部交易所產生的任何未變現收
          currency transaction gains or losses) arising from intra-              入及開支（外幣交易損益除外）均
          Group transactions, are eliminated. Unrealised losses                  予以抵銷。集團內交易產生的未
          resulting from intra-Group transactions are eliminated in              變現虧損僅在並無出現減值跡象
          the same way as unrealised gains, but only to the extent               的情況下以與未變現收益相同的
          that there is no evidence of impairment.                               方法予以抵銷。


          For each business combination, the Group can elect                      就各項業務合併而言，本集團可
          to measure any non-controlling interests (“NCI”) either                 選擇按公平值或按非控股權益

          the consolidated statement of financial position within                 控股權益。非控股權益於合併財

      (e)   Investments in debt and equity securities                         (e)   債務及股本證券投資
            The Group’s policies for investments in debt and equity                  本集團的債務及股本證券投資

            Investments in debt and equity securities are                           本集團在承諾購入╱出售投資當

            value plus directly attributable transaction costs, except              列賬，惟按公平值計量且其變動
            for those investments measured at fair value through                    計入損益的投資除外，該等投資
            profit or loss (FVPL) for which transaction costs are                   的交易成本直接於損益確認。有
            recognised directly in profit or loss. For an explanation               關本集團釐定金融工具公平值的
            of how the Group determines fair value of financial                     方法的闡述，請參閱附註22(f)。
            instruments, see note 22(f). These investments are                      該等投資其後根據其分類按以下

    (h)   Leased assets                                                       (h)   租賃資產
          At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                    於合約開始時，本集團評估合約
          the contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or                  是否屬於或包含租賃。倘合約賦
          contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right to                    予已識別資產在一段期間內的使
          control the use of an identified asset for a period of                    用控制權以換取代價，則該合約
          time in exchange for consideration. Control is conveyed                   屬於或包含租賃。倘客戶既有權
          where the customer has both the right to direct the use                   主導已識別資產的使用亦有權從
          of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all of                有關使用中獲取絕大部分經濟利
          the economic benefits from that use.                                      益，即賦予控制權。


          Where the contract contains lease component(s) and                        倘合約包含租賃部分及非租賃部
          non-lease component(s), the Group has elected not                         分，本集團已選擇不區分非租賃
          to separate non-lease components and accounts for                         部分，並就所有租賃將各租賃部
          each lease component and any associated non-lease                         分及任何相關非租賃部分以單一
          components as a single lease component for all leases.                    租賃部分入賬。


          At the lease commencement date, the Group recognises                      於租賃開始日期，本集團確認使
          a right-of-use asset and a lease liability, except for short-             用權資產及租賃負債，惟租期為
          term leases that have a lease term of 12 months or less                   12個月或以下的短期租賃及低價
          and leases of low-value assets. When the Group enters                     值資產租賃除外。當本集團就低
          into a lease in respect of a low-value asset, the Group                   價值資產訂立租賃時，本集團就
          decides whether to capitalise the lease on a lease-by-                    每份租賃決定是否進行資本化。
          lease basis. The lease payments associated with those                     未資本化租賃相關的租賃付款於
          leases which are not capitalised are recognised as an                     租期內有系統地確認為開支。
          expense on a systematic basis over the lease term.

          Where the lease is capitalised, the lease liability is                    倘租賃資本化，租賃負債初步按
          initially recognised at the present value of the lease                    租期內應付的租賃付款現值確
          payments payable over the lease term, discounted                          認，並使用租賃隱含的利率（或
          using the interest rate implicit in the lease or, if that                 倘該利率無法釐定，則使用相關
          rate cannot be readily determined, using a relevant                       增量借款利率）貼現。初步確認
          incremental borrowing rate. After initial recognition,                    後，租賃負債按攤銷成本計量，
          the lease liability is measured at amortised cost and                     利息開支則使用實際利率法計
          interest expense is calculated using the effective interest               算。不取決於指數或利率的可變
          method. Variable lease payments that do not depend on                     租賃付款並不計入租賃負債的計
          an index or rate are not included in the measurement of                   量，故於其產生的會計期間自損
          the lease liability and hence are charged to profit or loss               益扣除。
          in the accounting period in which they are incurred.

2   ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENT AND                                           2   會計判斷及估計
    ESTIMATES
    In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,            應用本集團會計政策的過程中，管理層
    management has made the following accounting judgements:               已作出以下會計判斷：

    (a)   Recognition of income taxes and deferred tax                     (a)   確認所得稅及遞延稅項資產
          assets
          Determining income tax provision involves judgement                    釐定所得稅撥備涉及對若干交易
          on the future tax treatment of certain transactions.                   的未來稅項處理的判斷。管理層
          Management evaluates tax implications of transactions                  評估交易的稅務影響並相應劃撥
          and tax provisions are set up accordingly. The tax                     稅項撥備。有關交易的稅務處理
          treatments of such transactions are reconsidered                       會定期重新檢討，並計及所有稅
          periodically to take into account all changes in tax                   法的變動。遞延稅項資產乃就可
          legislation. Deferred tax assets are recognised in respect             抵扣暫時性差異確認。由於該等
          of deductible temporary differences. As those deferred                 遞延稅項資產只能在可能有未來
          tax assets can only be recognised to the extent that it                應課稅溢利用於抵扣可抵扣暫時
          is probable that future taxable profits will be available              性差異的情況下才能確認，因此
          against which the deductible temporary differences                     需要管理層判斷以評估未來應課
          can be utilised, management’s judgement is required                    稅溢利的可能性。倘未來有可能
          to assess the probability of future taxable profits.                   產生應課稅溢利從而收回遞延稅
          Management’s assessment is revised as necessary                        項資產，管理層的評估於必要時
          and additional deferred tax assets are recognised if it                修訂及額外遞延稅項資產會予以
          becomes probable that future taxable profits will allow                確認。
          the deferred tax asset to be recovered.

          payments                                                               允價值

22 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND FAIR                                     22 財務風險管理及金融工具的
   VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS                                           公平值
   Exposure to credit risk, liquidity risk, interest rate risk, foreign      面對的信貸風險、流動性風險、利率風
   exchange risk and price risk arises in the normal course of the           險、外匯風險及價格風險於本集團的一
   Group’s business. The Group’s exposure to these risks and                 般業務過程中出現。本集團面臨該等風
   the financial risk management policies and practice used by               險及本集團管理該等風險所用的財務風
   the Group to manage these risks are described below.                      險管理政策及常規載列如下。

   (a)   Credit risk                                                         (a)   信貸風險
         Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will                   信貸風險指對手方未履行其合約
         default on its contractual obligations resulting in a                     責任導致本集團蒙受財務損失
         financial loss to the Group. The Group did not have                       的風險。本集團並無貿易應收款
         trade receivables. The counterparties of the Group’s                      項。本集團其他應收款項的對手
         other receivables are mainly the Group’s related parties,                 方主要為本集團的關聯方，管理
         management makes periodic assessments on the                              層根據歷史結算記錄及過往經驗
         recoverability of other receivables based on historical                   對其他應收款項的可收回性進行
         settlement records and past experience to accrue                          定期評估，以計提減值虧損（見
         impairment loss (see note 15(iii)). The exposure to credit                附註15(iii)）。面對的現金及現金
         risk arising from cash and cash equivalents is limited                    等價物所產生信貸風險有限，因
         because the counterparties are banks and financial                        為對手方為擁有低信貸風險的銀
         institutions with a low credit risk.                                      行及金融機構。

         The Group does not provide any guarantees which                           本集團概無提供將使本集團面臨
         would expose the Group to credit risk.                                    信貸風險的任何擔保。

   (b)   Liquidity risk                                                      (b)   流動性風險
         Liquidity risk is the risk that the Group will not be able                流動性風險指本集團將不能履行
         to meet its financial obligations as they fall due. The                   其到期財務責任的風險。本集團
         Group’s approach to managing liquidity is to ensure, as                   管理流動性的方針，是盡可能保
         far as possible, that it will have sufficient cash to meet                證其在正常或受壓情況下備有充
         its liabilities when they are due, under both normal and                  足現金，以償還到期負債，而不
         stressed conditions, without incurring unacceptable                       會產生過高損失或引致本集團聲
         losses or risking damage to the Group’s reputation.                       譽受損。

         The Group’s policy is to regularly monitor current and                    本集團的政策是定期監察現時及
         expected liquidity requirements, and to ensure that it                    預期流動資金需求，並確保其備
         maintains sufficient reserves of cash to meet its liquidity               有充足現金儲備，以滿足長短期
         requirements in the short and longer term.                                流動資金需求。

         The following tables show the remaining contractual                       下表列示於2022 年及2023 年年
         maturities at the end of 2022 and 2023 of the Group’s                     底按合約未貼現現金流量（包括
         financial liabilities, which are based on contractual                     按約定利率或在浮息情況下按報
         undiscounted cash flows (including interest payments                      告期末當時利率計算的利息款
         computed using contractual rates or, if floating, based                   項）計算本集團金融負債的合約
         on rates current at the end of the Reporting Period) and                  到期日剩餘期間以及本集團或須
         the earliest date the Group can be required to pay.                       付款的最早日期。

         because of changes in market interest rates. The interest                波動的風險。按公平值計量且其

         profit or loss is not significant due to the short-term                  利率風險不大，此乃由於該等金
         maturity of these financial instruments. Thus, the Group                 融工具到期日較短。因此，本集
         is not exposed to significant interest rate risk during the              團於2022 年及2023 年並無面臨
